Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on potentials structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 707. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 924 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_707(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 707. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^924(S, F) is trivial. QED.
